> **widget-tier spec ✅ DONE (J-453):** the N-059 concept-lock promoted to a formal spec — `ui/docs/xgen-widget-tier.md` (v1.0) + **D-102** + N-067. The `widget` = a **UI plugin**, the pluggable **Level-2** tier above the di/dd×atomic/composite grid (home `ui/common`). Same mechanism as a protocol/auth plugin, **one divergence**: the invocation channel is replaced by a **reactive `$common` store binding** (read + optional write-back). Checkable constraint set **W-1..W-11** — composes-down-only · owns state+lifecycle · I/O via declared seam only · one aggregate getter (publishes task-state, never payload/secret) · clean mount/unmount · skin-L2 pure/effect-separable · scoped home + Phase · honest phase-limits · representation (Svelte component + Level-2 envelope marker + `widgets/` home; static-import v1, registry/dynamic-mount reserved) · plugin contract · **dd-socket** (typed `$common` store handle + named slot, source-agnostic — defined ahead of any dd-component so one plugs in with zero widget rework). **Two-layer verify:** pure/presentational layer → sampler (5th **WIDGET** tab, mounted-not-`{#if}`); effect layer → real shell (D-097); one milestone, two verify homes. First **buildable** widget = `substitutions-editor` (M-RP4.3, dd-free); `temperature-indicator` first **conceived** but **dd-blocked**. Spec is **v1.0 first-instance-provisional** — firms once M-RP4.3 proves it. Design-only, no code.

> **M-RP4.3 `substitutions-editor` ✅ DONE (J-454):** the **FIRST widget** built + closed. A settings widget that edits the `[substitutions]` rules string (one textarea, D-100 1:1) → the `$common` store → live text-processor morph; explicit Apply/Revert gated on `dirty && valid`; getter `{dirty,valid,count}`. **Two verify homes (D-102):** pure layer → sampler (Chat, 5th WIDGET tab, registry 93→97, state machine + live cross-widget morph + Revert, 0 orphans); persistence → real shell **seam-only** (Clair, `set_substitutions` command + `write_substitutions_section` helper, strict write +4 tests 131→135; direct-`invoke` at 9222, on-disk write + relaunch clean-slate→seed = session-only under D-101). The widget is **not** mounted in the client shell (no content layer yet — Joe-lock Option 2); logic/UI → sampler, persistence → real shell. **Spec firmed to v1.1:** W-3 (a `common` widget host-injects shell I/O via a callback, never a bare `@tauri-apps/api` import — first-instance build finding) + W-8 (first-run-no-config caveat). **Seed fix:** `-->`/`<--` → `->`/`<-` — the `--` rule substring-shadows `-->` on live keystroke-rescan (D-100 amendment). Next-active → M-RP4.1 (kind-3 number-clamp).

> **M-RP4.1 kind-3 filter/guard ✅ DONE (J-455):** `number` min/max **clamp** — the 2nd of four processor kinds built (D-099). `ClampRule {min?,max?}` + `applyClamp` (pure, idempotent, `number|null` pass-through) in `transform.ts` + a change-triggered `clamp.ts` attachment (sibling of the input-shaped `processor.ts`) + `number` as first **clamp-host** (`{...rest}` additive). Coerces on **commit** (`change`), not per-keystroke. Pure layer only — no Rust, fully sampler-verified (9422: `99→10`/`-5→0`/in-range no-op, registry 97→98, 0 orphans). Next-active → kind 2 (converter/bridge, `Intl`) → kind 4 (`use:render`, deferred) → dd-components.

> **M-RP4.5 kind-2 converter/bridge ✅ DONE (J-456):** `converter-field` — the **25th `core`** and the one processor kind (D-099) that ships as a **component, not an attachment**. Kinds 1/3 forward an attachment and sync through a single same-type `bind:value`; kind 2 has **two reps of different type** (formatted display string + typed bound `T`), so it is a host component owning both. Pure DOM-free core in `transform.ts` (`PARSE_FAILED` symbol + `Converter<T> {toString,fromString,toEditable?}` + first concrete `intlNumber(opts?,locale?)` over `Intl.NumberFormat`, `formatToParts`-derived parser); host = new di atomic (`<script generics="T">`, root `<input type=text>`, owns `value`/`text`/`invalid`, DEV `__XGEN_CONVERT__`). Timing: parse on `change`/`blur`, `focus`→raw `toEditable`, nothing on `input` (decoupled → no caret-restore). Parse-fail = **reject-and-mark** (`[data-invalid]`, value held); empty = no-op revert. **Tier-1 only** (converter is code, not a user string — no caps/lint). Pure layer only, fully sampler-verified (9422, both accents: pure-core round-trip + `PARSE_FAILED`, live blur-format, `abc`→data-invalid+value-held+border `--err`, empty-revert; registry **98→100**; 0 orphans). Next-active → kind 4 (`use:render`, deferred) → dd-components.

> **M-RP2.30 `meter` ✅ DONE (J-457):** the **26th `core`** and **5th simple display-di** (after label/paragraph/image/led), root native `<meter>` — the read-only sibling of `range` (same `{value,min,max}` shape, opposite direction). **di, not dd** (a bare value+range is a display primitive; the "first dd" framing was corrected). `value` plain prop (read-only), `optimum?`/`low?`/`high?` drive the native semantic fill; getter `{value,min,max,optimum}`; no caption (consumer adds it). **Full-width by default** (`display:block`+`width:100%`) + optional `width?` pin + `min-width:80px`. Founds **`--warn: #ba7517`** (L2 amber, sub-optimum). Pseudo-heavy `.meter` skin (PROVISIONAL): `::-webkit-meter-optimum-value` `--ok` / `-suboptimum-value` `--warn` / `-even-less-good-value` `--err`. Build finding: no-`optimum` bar paints green (not neutral grey — native limitation). Sampler-verified (9422, N-042 rule-inspection + screenshot): registry **100→106**, getters exact, `METER`/block/full-width + `width:120px` fixed + opacity disabled, `--warn` resolves, accent-neutral, 0 orphans. Next-active → dd track opens (`section-header` ungrounded → `entity-avatar` domain-bound, D-071-audited); `temperature-indicator` later consumes meter via the widget dd-socket.

> **M-RP2.30a `meter` `fill?` ✅ DONE (J-458):** additive amendment — an optional **`fill?`** prop (hex or `var(--token)`) sets an inline `--meter-fill` var; the three value-pseudos become `var(--meter-fill, var(--ok|--warn|--err))`, so a set `fill` **overrides the semantic fills entirely** (the led/chip inline-var mechanism, Joe-lock option A); unset = unchanged. Getter gains `fill`; composes with `width?` in one inline style. Closes the no-optimum-green gap (`fill="var(--t3)"` = neutral). Sampler-verified (9422): `#custom {…,fill:"var(--accent2)"}` + inline var, pseudos read `var(--meter-fill, …)`, registry **106→108**, 0 orphans. Next-active → dd track opens.

> **M-RP2.31 `section` ✅ DONE (J-459):** the **27th `core`**, root native `<section>` — a **collapsible disclosure container** (di, binding none; **atomic-ish** — composes no registering child components, one getter per instance). Optional header `<h{level} class="section-header">` (title + `badge?: string` programmatic + chevron) over a `<div class="section-body">` `children` slot. `collapsible?`/`collapsed?` ($bindable) hide the body via `[data-collapsed]` `display:none` (slot stays mounted); **nesting is free** (a `section` in the body → its own id). `<div>`-body (honest neutral container for arbitrary/nestable content); **solid header band** (future bg-colour/picture widget-mods). Chevron **reuses combobox's `--tri`/`--tri-open` masked glyphs**, swapped on collapse. Filter/search deferred (a panel/widget concern, not this component). **Supersedes the seed `section-header`** (⬛ DEPRECATED). Getter `{title, badge, collapsible, collapsed}`. Sampler-verified (9422): registry **108→114**, getters exact, `SECTION`/`<h2>`, `#bare` headerless, `#nested` body holds a child `<section>`, collapse → `[data-collapsed]`/`aria-expanded=false`/body `display:none`/chevron swap, 12 `.section*` rules in cascade, 0 orphans. Next-active → the dd track opens for real (`entity-avatar` domain-bound, D-071-audited: IdentityRecord/SpaceState → `container-list-item` → `spaces-panel` composes `section` + rows).

> **M-RP2.31a `section` `width?` ✅ DONE (J-460):** additive amendment — an optional **`width?`** prop (string, e.g. `"320px"`) → inline `width` on the root `<section>`; unset = **100%** (fills container). `.section` gains `min-width:160px` + `box-sizing:border-box`. Getter gains `width` (meter precedent). Sampler-verified (9422): `#fixed {…,width:"320px"}` + computed `width:320px`, `#plain` unset + `min-width:160px`, registry **114→115**, 0 orphans. Next-active → dd track opens.

> **M-RP5.0 `entity-avatar` ✅ DONE (J-462):** the **dd track opens for real** — the **first data-dependent component** (dd-**atomic**), the first `data-dependent/` occupant. Materializes ONE address-book entry (identity or space) into a visual; **shape branches on the data** (dd ≠ di): identity + DM space = circle, non-DM space = rounded-square. **The dd-root rule (N-075):** dd does NOT inherit the di `<div>`=composite litmus — root = honest HTML for the materialized thing (`<figure role="img">`, `aria-label={name ?? kind}`, `<figcaption>` reserved for `labeled`/`card`). Source-agnostic **`EntityDescriptor { kind, name?, id, flags{isAi?,revoked?,isDm?,e2e?}, image? }`** seam = the **W-11 dd-socket** payload; `core` imports **no** `IdentityRecord`/`SpaceState` (shell owns the protocol → descriptor map). Colour = shared **`seedColour(name ?? id)`** base helper **factored out of `chip`** (byte-identical, chip re-verified 0-regression; no `--accent` dependency → shell-independent). `initials` = 1–2 graphemes of `name` (grapheme-safe), absent → xgid-tail fallback. Primary axis = **`variant`** (purpose): `presence` (xs, glyph) / `list` (sm, + initials) — size/content derived. Self-drawn badges (not a nested `led`): `isAi` `::after` violet spark, `revoked` `grayscale`+`::before` slash. `onActivate?` reserved (menu seam, M-RP5.3). Getter `{ kind, variant, name, initials, seed, flags }`. Sampler DD·atomic panel populated, CDP-verified (9422): registry **115→124** (9 cells), FIGURE/role=img, shape-per-kind, `absent→"AZ"` fallback, seed client↔node identical (`seedMatch:true`), 8 `.entity-avatar*` rules in cascade, chip 0-regression, 0 orphans. Next-active → `container-list-item` (dd-composite, composes entity-avatar; unlocks `labeled`/`card`) → `spaces-panel` → `temperature-indicator` widget. Track A (J-461) gates the status-bearing avatar variants (M-RP5.2).

> **M-RP5.1 `entity-item` ✅ DONE (J-463):** the **first dd-composite** (renamed from `container-list-item`), the second `data-dependent/` occupant — a variant-driven display unit that materializes an address-book entry (identity ∪ space ∪ DM) as avatar + name + optional secondary + trailing meta/status. Root `<div class="entity-item">` (N-075). **Single-knob derive:** the consumer sets one `entity-item` `variant` (`row`/`card`/`nav`/`inline`); the composite derives the inner `entity-avatar` variant (`row→list · card→card · nav→labeled · inline→presence`). Slot surface derived per variant (row = name+meta · card = name+secondary+status · nav/inline = name); secondary/status/meta = **caller-supplied slots** (source-agnostic; shell maps protocol + Track A `state.status`). Composes the **real** `entity-avatar` child (self-registers `__avatar` → the matrix multiplies, status-indicator precedent). **Global width rule (N-076):** unset → 100%, set → value, `min-width` = slot-composition floor (retro-references `meter`/`section`, which already ship the shape). **`entity-avatar` amendment (additive, pre-announced at M-RP5.0):** `variant` widened `+ labeled | card` (render initials at ascending sizes list 28 / labeled 32 / card 40; 0-regression on presence/list). Sampler DD·composite panel populated, CDP-verified (9422): registry **124→138** (+14 = 7 composites + 7 `__avatar` children), 0 orphans, derive-map literal, width unset→180 floor / set→280px, root `DIV.entity-item`, 14 `.entity-item*` rules in cascade, card density border + `--s2`. Next-active → `spaces-panel` (composes `section` + `entity-item ×N`, owns roving focus) → `entity-context-menu` widget (consumes `onActivate?`) → `temperature-indicator` widget (W-11 dd-socket). Track A gates the status-bearing variants (M-RP5.2).

> **M-RP5.1a `status` + M-RP5.1b avatar corner-slot ✅ DONE (J-464):** the self-status **dd-atomic** (the 2nd, after `entity-avatar`) + the additive `entity-avatar` `status?` corner-slot. Materializes Track A `state.status` (J-461) — personal **expression** (emoji + short line), NOT presence — via a source-agnostic view-model `{emoji?,text?,updatedAt?,expiresAt?}` (`core` imports no protocol type). **Variants** `badge` (emoji corner) / `line` (emoji + text) / `full` (+ relative "updated Nm ago"). Root `<span class="status" role="img">` (badge) / `<span class="status">` (line/full); getter `{variant,emoji,hasText,expired}`; `title` tooltip fallback. **Mounted-but-empty on expiry (Joe-locked, corrects the runbook's "not rendered"):** the root `<span use:envelope>` always mounts (getter always registers, `expired:true` CDP-readable), rendering no content when expired/content-less — `data-empty` collapses it. **Avatar seam (5.1b):** `entity-avatar` gains `status?`, rendering `<Status variant="badge" id=__status>` as a bottom-right corner overlay (child self-registers; avatar getter unchanged → 0-regression). **Collision fix (D-065):** combobox/tag-select popups already use a bare ad-hoc `.status`; the base rule is scoped `.status[data-variant]` (the component always emits it; the popups never do) → no leak into those closed components. Sampler DD·atomic cells + avatar-with-status, CDP-verified (9422): registry **138→146** (+8 = 6 standalone + `entity-avatar#with-status` + its `__status`), 0 orphans; expired registers `{expired:true}` on a collapsed span; `full` = "🎧 in a meeting updated 5m ago"; corner child `position:absolute -3px` inside the avatar; avatar/item cells unchanged; bare-`.status` probe `display:inline` (leak gone). Next-active → `spaces-panel` (M-RP5.2, inherits status via the avatar) → `entity-context-menu` widget (uses `status full`).

> **M-RP5.2 `entity-panel` ✅ DONE (J-465):** the **last dd-composite** — closes the dd-composite tier (widget tier next). A roving-focus, single-select **listbox** that materializes a list of entries: a `section` (group chrome, self-registers `__section`) wrapping a `<ul role="listbox">` of `entity-item` option rows; the panel owns the keyboard focus + selection + empty state (which `entity-item` deferred to it). `spaces-panel` is a consumer **preset** (title + spaces items), not a separate component. **Rooting ("wrap, not beside"):** the outermost DOM is the `<section>`; the panel's own getter rides the `<ul class="entity-panel" use:envelope>` inside — the panel IS the listbox. **Focus:** roving tabindex (one row `0`, rest `-1`), Arrows/Home/End move + focus, Enter/Space + click select+activate; `selected` ($bindable id) → `aria-selected` + the row's `[data-selected]`. Empty → a composed `paragraph` message + `hasEmpty:true`; collapse pass-through to `section` hides rows (slot mounted). Getter `{count,selected,collapsed,hasEmpty}`. **+Corner-fix H (skin-only):** `entity-avatar` isAi spark → **top-right** (was bottom-right), de-colliding with the bottom-right status badge. **+`entity-item` status-forward amendment (D-065):** a variant shows status **once** — `card` = inline line (no avatar badge), `row`/`nav`/`inline` = avatar corner badge (`avatarStatus = variant==='card' ? undefined : status`); the wiring Phase-0's "for free" assumed but M-RP5.1 never added; `card-space` 0-regression verified. Sampler DD·composite cells, CDP-verified (9422): getters exact (bind:selected seeded); registry **146→173** (+27), 0 orphans; `role=listbox`/`option`; roving tabindex `["-1","0","-1"]`; ArrowDown moves active+focus, Enter sets `selected`+`aria-selected`; empty "No blocked users."; collapse `display:none` (rows mounted); Aria avatar isAi `top:-1px` + status `bottom:-3px` (no overlap); `card-space` no corner badge + inline "🟢 online" intact. **dd-composite tier CLOSED.** Next → the widget tier: `entity-context-menu` (M-RP5.3, the 100% read, uses `status full`) → `temperature-indicator` (M-RP5.4, `meter` via the W-11 dd-socket).

⚫ **Track A — self-set status protocol arc — CLOSED (J-461).** A parallel protocol arc opened while the dd track was scoped: self-set status (emoji + description line) was found to be entirely absent from the protocol (audit of `IdentityRecord`/`SpaceState` — only system-derived `revoked`/`is_ai`/`member_temperature`/`active_mutes` exist). Closed in three doc phases + one impl: **PROTO-STATUS.0 ✅** (dedicated `state.status` object, not on `IdentityRecord`; shape `{emoji?,text?,updated_at,expires_at?}`, global/public, presence excluded) → **PROTO-STATUS.1 ✅** (key `state.status/<identity_xgid>`, caps: 1-grapheme emoji / ≤128B text / `expires_at` now+60s..30d, clear-by-delete, lazy expiry) → **PROTO-STATUS.2 ✅** (Clair reference impl: new `xgen-core/src/status/` module, `StatusRecord` + validating ctor + `is_expired`, reuse `state.*` resolution + owner-write guard, 19 tests; 1502 passed / 0 failed workspace, clippy clean; Appendix I §V.4 entry, J-461). ## Cross-cutting

A few items don't fit cleanly in past / present / near future / far future because they are continuous rather than milestone-shaped. Recorded here for visibility.

🟢 **Design discipline (D-069).** Every milestone Phase 0 must be Joe-locked before the implementing phase starts. Delegated technical drafts must self-flag open items. Canonical-document rule: each major surface gets one authoritative document, others point at it. M6's Phase 0 was the first milestone to follow this discipline end-to-end; Federation Event Propagation's design phase (Pass 2 just closed, Pass 3 next) is the second instance of the same pattern.

✅ **Audit-precedes-dependency discipline (D-071).** Every future milestone's Phase 0 includes a subsystem audit of whatever the milestone depends on. The Propagation Reliability Audit (J-081) established the pattern; D-071 names the discipline. Pairs with D-069: audit phase → design phase → implementation phase, each producing a canonical artefact. Sibling to D-065 and D-070. Promoted to DECISIONS.md 2026-05-18.

🟢 **Honest behaviour over polite behaviour (D-065).** Protocol-design principle. When the system can choose between a behaviour that misrepresents its state and one that honestly reflects it, XGen picks honest. Surfaces in multiple places: AI Client drop-on-throttle pacing, Node event rejection clarity, mute semantics, M6 accept-signal design, Federation Pass 2's `sync_complete` lock (F-6 chose explicit signal over silent quiet-time heuristic citing D-065), Federation Pass 2's pagination lock (F-7 chose explicit cursor over "felt incomplete" heuristic citing D-065).

✅ **Two events of equal importance, opposite direction (D-070).** Protocol-design principle. When the protocol exposes a signal from one party to another about the outcome of an action, both directions of outcome (acceptance and rejection) must be exposed with equal first-class status, AND both directions must carry the envelope-level correlation identifier so the originator can correlate the signal to the action it sent. Sibling to D-065. Promoted to DECISIONS.md 2026-05-18 with corrected post-audit framing (both halves — existence AND correlation — are load-bearing).

✅ **Bidirectional sustainability discipline (D-077).** Audit/design-phase principle. At every silent-discard, conditional-mutation, or fallible-operation-with-discard pattern, the sustainability question MUST be asked in both directions: forward-drift (what future callers could bypass this guard) AND backward-coherence (what current callers depend on this as a feature). Both answered simultaneously before locking any fix in isolation. Sits at meta-layer above the no-drift-surface discipline family (D-067 + D-070 + D-075 + D-076 v1.1 at protocol layers; D-077 + Rule 0 at meta layer). Origin: J-107 persistence-amendment re-walk — J-105 design phase asked the forward-only sustainability question and locked Q1 at (a).iii.β (`Result<(), GraphError>` ingest_event); Clair's Commit 2 implementation surfaced cross-milestone Phase 7 B3 amendment dependency (B3 federation-bootstrap path implicitly relied on the silent-discard as a feature); bidirectional discipline would have caught it at design time. Resolution Option Y locked: revert (a).iii.β → (a).iii.α, name the discipline as new principle, document broader audit as future-walk material under candidate D-NNN expanded scope. Surface-driven application per D-071 — NOT applied preemptively to all silent-discard sites in the codebase; applied at each fix site as it surfaces. **First worked instance** of D-077 value: Clair's Commit 3 prospective sweep closed three within-Commit-3 audit gaps atomically (abort-fold + identity-registry-persist + space-event-store-persist; federation-registry-persist audited and confirmed safe). Promoted to DECISIONS.md 2026-05-24 at J-107.

✅ **XGID typing is wire-format and persistence-format invariant (D-081).** Data-model / wire-format principle. Retyping a `String` identifier slot to a typed XGID flavour is a pure in-memory change: every flavour is `#[serde(transparent)]` over `Xgid(String)`, so it serialises byte-identically on every boundary (Node↔Node, Node↔Client, AI-control / batch JSONL, on-disk). No XGID Retrofit pass (1–5) changed a single serialized byte. `Display` is the canonical string form; `Debug` reveals the wrapper for diagnostics only. Sibling to D-076 in the wire-format discipline family; realises the XGID Adoption v1 Q4 invariance promise. Promoted to DECISIONS.md 2026-05-29 at J-148 (arc close). Numbered D-081 — D-080 was already taken by the Node-storage EventStore decision.

✅ **"operator" reserved for the AI-operator role; the Node administrator is a distinct infra principal (D-082).** Protocol-vocabulary / naming principle. "operator" = the AI-operator role only (moderator-parallel: operator : AI-identities :: moderator : room + members; fall-upward per D-064) — never an owner/admin alias. The Node admin principal is the **administrator** (prose) / **admin** (code, CLI, error-codes, config — matching `admin_ops`/`AdminContext`/`AdminError`); v1 has no gradation (OS-user-equals-administrator, session-scoped). owner/super-admin reserved as a future sub-tier (M7). A Node administrator auto-administers Spaces it originates/homes but NOT federated-in replicas (hosts-but-doesn't-own); the signing identity for admin-originated Space events is deferred to the A4 sub-design. Sibling to D-073 (naming discipline). Recorded at J-149; scope-refined at J-150 after a corpus audit found "operator" carries four senses — only the runtime admin principal (Sense D) is renamed; the AI-operator role, the wire field names (`operator_display_name` etc.), and the infrastructure "Node operator"/data-controller sense are all kept (an inline facet-specifier disambiguates Sense C where needed).

🟢 **Honest longer work over fast shortcuts.** Project-management principle. When project work surfaces a real gap, the default response is to close the gap properly, even if that delays downstream work. Locked during the audit's federation finding discussion; informs all milestone-sequencing calls. Pairs with the audit-precedes-dependency discipline above. Federation Pass 2 invoked it three times — to fold in F-6 (sync_complete) rather than defer, to fold in F-7 (pagination) rather than defer, and to fold in F-10 (HeldPending generalisation) rather than reject. Within Federation Event Propagation milestone scope as of J-105 the principle has eight recurrences (Phase 7.5; bidirectional; topo-sort design close J-097; runbook landing J-098; re-walk Step 2 J-099; re-walk Step 3 J-100; topo-sort implementation J-101; persistence-amendment sub-amendment milestone surfacing at J-104). Design-close events do NOT increment the count; the count belongs to the milestone-event the recurrence opened.

🟡 **Candidate D-NNN — "Ingest path invariant encoding"** (flagged at J-105, NOT promoted to DECISIONS.md; **scope expanded at J-107 re-walk** to cover five `ingest_event` silents + three drain helpers + M6 reject paths + B3 apply_event dependency). The persistence-amendment design phase locked Q1 at (a).iii.β (type-level Result-returning `ingest_event`); Y-lock revert at J-107 reverted to (a).iii.α (binary-void signature + log-level vigilance) under cross-milestone Phase 7 B3 amendment dependency surfaced at Clair's Commit 2. The candidate D-NNN names the rung-above-(a).iii.α project-level open question without pre-committing the project to a specific shape; rungs above (a).iii.β named explicitly (ValidatedEvent wrapper, sealed traits + visitor pattern, formal verification). Sibling-shape to D-076's v1 → v1.1 progression at different scope: v1 design-close didn't pre-promote the second invariant; v1.1 emerged after design walked it properly. Resolution at J-107 re-walk close: ship (a).iii.α immediately + name D-077 discipline + flag candidate D-NNN with **expanded scope** preserving optionality on the right rung per D-069 audit-vs-design boundary discipline. Future walk triggered when (a) dependent work surfaces a concrete drift instance, OR (b) Joe locks the candidate as worth pursuing on philosophical/strategic grounds independent of a surfacing gap. See JOURNAL J-105 + J-107 entries + `tasks/PHASE_7_5_PERSISTENCE_AMENDMENT_DESIGN.md` v1.2 §8 for the full reasoning trail.

## How to read this document

A reader landing here for the first time should be able to answer three questions in under a minute:

1. **What has the project shipped?** Past section, scanned by reading the bold milestone names.
2. **What is being worked on right now?** Present section.
3. **What's next?** Near future, top-of-list.

A reader returning after a gap can scan for state-changes by looking for symbols that have moved (a 🟢 PLAY that became ✅ DONE, a 🟡 PENDING that became 🟢 PLAY, a new 🟡 entry that wasn't there before). The roadmap is meant to be scannable, not exhaustively read.

For any item the reader wants more detail on, the canonical source is named (JOURNAL entry, DECISIONS reference, design doc, task file). ROADMAP.md is the map; the territory lives elsewhere.

---

*End of roadmap.*
